BUTTON LIST:
──────────────────────────────────────────
  SCAN  — Reads the code of every script in the selected places.
  SMART — Snapshot + captured data + game mechanics + diagnostics, saved as a .txt file.
  FULL  — Everything in Smart plus index, remotes, tree and config values.
  MECH  — Game mechanics summary (damage, shop, quests...). Copied to clipboard.
  INDEX — List of scripts and whether they are readable, empty or obfuscated.
  PATHS — Full path of every scanned script.
  REMOTES — All RemoteEvents / RemoteFunctions found in the game.
  CONFIG — Value objects and attributes (prices, cooldowns, ratios).
  TREE  — Folder structure of the main services (2-3 levels).
